| E296 | The media URL is used with a malformed, repeated, tampered or other tenant's token, a wrong position, after expiry, after the message failed, was canceled or delivered, after erasure, or past the per-item limit | The same 404 every time, for GET and HEAD (same status, code and headers). The token is in no API log line, audit row or response | R-MMS-6 |
| E297 | The agent loses the lead before their scheduled photo text is due (E15) | Blocked with `SENDER_NOT_AUTHORIZED`. The new owner's suggested draft has the words only and says the photos were not carried over. A photo-only text (no words, or only the brokerage line) creates no draft | R-MMS-1, R-MMS-5 |
| E298 | The submit times out (dispatch_unknown) | The media URLs stay valid while the reconcile decides. If it is unresolved after 10 minutes, the message is failed and its URLs stop answering. Two photo texts to the same number in that window with the same words, or with none, cannot be told apart, and both end "we couldn't confirm it went out"; neither is ever resent. 24 h later, the provider's copies are found by the sending number and the time and deleted, with a P2 alert if none is found | R-MMS-6, R-MMS-7, R-GATE-8 |
| E299 | The person is erased, or an unused upload reaches 24 h | Erasure shreds the person's sent media and staged uploads in its transaction, and the 24 h cleanup deletes the provider's copies. The purge job shreds an unused upload | R-MMS-3, R-MMS-7, R-DATA-4 |
| E300 | Sam's iPhone answers the team's "See you at 3" with `Loved “See you at 3”` | Linked (love, add): ❤️ shows under that bubble, with the line "Sam loved “See you at 3”". It pauses, hands back once, and makes the feed card and the notification ("Sam R. reacted to a text") exactly as a reply | R-REACT-1, R-REACT-2, R-REACT-4, R-REACT-6 |
| E301 | Texts that each quote a text the team sent from that number to that phone: `Liked “Let me know if you don’t want any more texts about this one”`, `Loved “No more texts about Oak St, I promise. Talk soon!”`, `Questioned “Wrong number?”`, `Removed a heart from “Do not text after 9”` | None is an opt-out or a wrong number: no consent event, no person-level do-not-contact, no point marked. Each is audited `consent.reaction_quote_not_read`. The notification adds "Not read as an opt-out or a wrong number: check it". The thread says the quoted words come from a text sent to Sam and offers "They asked not to be contacted…" (two taps). The next text is allowed | R-REACT-3 |
| E302 | The same texts, but no stored text matches: the quoted text went to another phone, to another contact or from another team number, was sent 31 days ago, was imported, blocked or failed, or was never sent | Not a reaction: classified exactly as before (person-level do-not-contact with needs_review, or wrong number), and reviewable with "Not an opt-out" | R-REACT-2, R-REACT-3 |
| E303 | Extra words around a reaction, for example `Loved “See you at 3” please stop texting me`, or any text before the verb | Not a reaction, because the parse is anchored. The whole text is classified as before, so the written opt-out counts | R-REACT-1, R-REACT-3 |
| E304 | Google Messages and Spanish forms: `👍 to “See you at 3”` with U+200B around the emoji and U+200A inside the quotes; the older straight-quote forms with or without hair spaces; `👍 a “…”`; `Se quitó 👍 de "…"` and `Se ha quitado 👍 de "…"`; `Le encantó “…”`; the iPhone form `Reacted ‼️ to “…”` | Each links like E300. The emoji is kept as received, joiners, tags and variation selectors included | R-REACT-1, R-REACT-2 |
| E305 | A removal (`Removed a heart from “…”`, `Removed 👍 from “…”`, `Dejó de encantarle “…”`) or a changed reaction | The chip is cleared, or replaced by the later add. A line says "Sam removed a heart from “…”". It is still a reply ("Sam R. removed a reaction from a text") | R-REACT-6, R-REACT-4 |
| E306 | A media reaction (`Loved an image`, `👍 to a photo`, `Le gustó una imagen`) | Not parsed: no quote proves whose words they are. It is an ordinary text, classified exactly as before | R-REACT-1, R-REACT-2 |
| E307 | The quote is cut (for example ending in "…") or a carrier changed a character (VERIFY 1, 9) | Linked only if it still equals a text sent to that phone, ignoring case, spacing, apostrophes and double quotes. Otherwise it is not a reaction and is classified as before | R-REACT-2 |
| E308 | A reaction-shaped text that arrives through web chat or email, as MMS with media, in imported FUB history, from an agent's alert phone, from an unknown number, or from a phone shared by two contacts that lands on the contact the text was not sent to (two people; records of one person merged together are one contact here, R-REACT-2) | Never a reaction: handled and classified exactly as before | R-REACT-1, R-REACT-2 |
| E309 | The assistant drafts after a reaction | The prompt shows "client (sms reaction to the team's text [Sn])" with only the reaction as data. A claim that the client said the team's words fails verification. A quoted price makes neither the draft nor the approved reply "money" | R-REACT-7 |
| E310 | A website-chat lead's agent answers in the open chat before the first-contact deadline | The reply is the period's first touch, written at dispatch TX1 as a text's is (`first_touch_recorded`, `FIRST_TOUCH_CHAT`): no breach, no `untouched_lead`, speed-to-lead answered with its response time, no auto-release, and the first-contact card ("Goes back to the pond … if not contacted") leaves the feed. The scoreboard counts it once as a touch and its period as answered, live and from the rollup. A chat reply after a re-inquiry that started a new period is that period's first touch, never the earlier miss's late fix (R-ACC-2, E68). Owner decision D8 (2026-11-12) | R-OWN-14, R-ACC-2, R-ACC-4 |
| E311 | The assistant answered in the chat first; a Team Lead, then a collaborator, reply in the chat | The assistant's first response is never a touch (R-AST-15). Who counts is the rule of a text: a collaborator's chat reply is the touch; a teammate who is neither the owner nor a collaborator (a Team Lead) may reply, but that is not a touch and the SLA keeps running. The Team Lead's reply answers the client's message, so the owner's waiting card goes, but the owner's first-contact card still leads with Reply in the chat (never "Can't reach them" for a chat-only lead). If the deadline passes, the item says "No human call, text, email or chat reply from the agent or a collaborator", which stays true | R-OWN-14, R-AST-15, R-ACC-2 |
| E312 | The agent's chat reply is refused: the visitor left (the chat ended), the widget was disconnected, or the person asked not to be contacted | Blocked in TX1 (`CHANNEL_NOT_CONNECTED`, `PERSON_DO_NOT_CONTACT`), with its `gate_decisions` row, and never a touch: the lead is breached and auto-released at its deadlines like any untouched lead. A reply the gate allowed whose delivery then fails stays the touch (its instant is TX1), as a text's does, and raises `failed_send`. The chat has ended only once the visitor has gone 30 minutes without a heartbeat (E77): a reply 29 minutes after their last poll is sent and is the touch, though they may have closed the page and never read it | R-OWN-14, R-INBOX-7, R-CHAN-3, E77 |
| E341 | A person the FUB migration brought in unassigned (owner decision B4: their mapped member was suspended) inquires again: a portal post, a partner feed, a lead email or the generic intake | Owner decision D8 (2026-11-12; batch 6 review ownership-sla/F4). Before it, the inquiry was attached and nothing else happened. Now the inquiry is recorded on them (`reinquiry`; no second contact) and they are routed like a returning lead: `reinquiry` `REINQUIRY_ROUTE`, then the routing rules as the system actor, an offer to an eligible member with its timer, `lead_offer` notification and feed card, or a pond. The suspended member is never the target: the rule considers and skips them (`MEMBERSHIP_INACTIVE`); a rule naming only them sends the person to the pond with `NO_ELIGIBLE_AGENT` and the critical notice to Team Leads and Admins. The accept starts the person's own first-touch period (R-OWN-13). No first response. The import report says when they asked again (the inquiry's time, in the team's time) and where the routing took them, and "Assign these people" no longer selects them (E231). One who asked before the ownership commit was routed only once the owners were in (E343), and is counted and described the same way. B4 is otherwise unchanged: nothing is assigned to a suspended member, the migration continues, the report lists who came in unassigned | R-OWN-20, R-OWN-11, R-IMPORT-5 |
| E342 | An unassigned lead awaiting the router (a route is due: after a decline, an expiry, a reopen), or a lead on offer, inquires again; or two inquiries from the same unassigned person arrive at once | Unchanged: the inquiry is attached only (`attached`) and the router routes the lead once; no re-inquiry event and no second routing. Two simultaneous inquiries of one person serialize on the address lock (same phone or email) or on the contact lock (different ones), which intake takes before reading the state, so the second sees what the first did. When the first routing makes an offer, the second attaches. When it sends the person to a pond (nobody eligible, a pond rule), the second follows the pond's setting like any pond lead: by default a second routing (`REINQUIRY_REROUTE`), and with nobody eligible the pond again, with the critical notice again (review D8-R4; whether a pond entry seconds old should hold a re-inquiry back is an open question for the owner). A merge that lands between intake's match and its lock is followed to the record the person lives on now (review D8-R5) | R-OWN-20, R-OWN-25 |
| E343 | Any other unassigned person with no route due inquires again: a FUB person with no owner, a CSV row, two imported people merged before the owners were imported, a person put back by an ownership-stage undo; or a person the contacts stage brought in who asks again before the ownership commit; or a live lead (on offer, in a pond, awaiting the router) is merged into such a person | Routed like E341, with two exceptions. Before the ownership commit (review D8-R1) the person is in the import's creation state and their Follow Up Boss owner is mapped and approved but not yet imported: the inquiry is attached only (`attached`; no routing, offer or notice), so the approved mapping decides who holds them, and the import replays it once the owners are in (job `imports.replay_inquiries` when the ownership stage commits, is skipped, or a revert ends the wait): the mapped member owns them as planned (the record is imported, nothing to document) and hears the inquiry (`REINQUIRY`, a notice and a card); one whose member cannot hold leads (B4) or who has no owner is routed then, like E341, and the report says when they asked (the inquiry's time) and to whom. A contacts undo keeps a person who asked again since (touched, R-IMPORT-10) and then routes them. A live lead merged into such a person (review D8-R3) gives the survivor a route due now (the follow-up routes it once; the agent the loser was on offer to is told the offer was withdrawn), or, from a pond, that pond | R-OWN-20, R-OWN-24, R-IMPORT-5, R-IMPORT-7, R-IMPORT-10 |
| E344 | A sell-side deal records the buyer (another agent's client) as `buyer`; a buy-side deal records the seller as `seller` | The team's milestone updates go only to the team's side (R-TXN-4, K1): no firing is planned for the other side's client, and the deal screen says "the buyer is not this team's client on a sell deal"; a dual deal sends to both sides | R-TXN-4 |
| E345 | A lease deal with a `buyer`-role contact, and its primary contact (`other`) | No automatic milestone update to anyone (owner default); the deal page says lease deals get no automatic updates | R-TXN-4 |
| E346 | After an update is planned the TC corrects a recipient's role to the other side's client role | Re-read live when the update is built: skipped `NOT_OUR_CLIENT` with its sentence, nothing sent | R-TXN-4 |
| E347 | A past seller whose home is the house they sold; a renter on a closed lease deal; a deal's primary contact with no client role; the other side's seller on a buy deal | No valuation is requested and no home-value note fires (K2) | R-AVM-3 |
| E348 | A past buyer's recorded home matches a house they sold through the team (before or after the note was planned) | Never valued; a note planned before the sale closed is skipped `ADDRESS_NOT_CURRENT` when it is built | R-AVM-3 |
| E349 | A deal recipient is merged after the reminder pass planned their update: before it went out, or after it was sent | Before: the loser's run is skipped (`CONTACT_REMOVED`) and the next pass plans it once for the survivor, who now holds the role; after: nothing is sent again and the deal screen says the update was sent to a record since merged (never "nothing planned") | R-OWN-24, R-TXN-4 |
| E350 | Two records of one person are on the same deal, the survivor as `other` (or `lender`, or the other side's client role) and the loser as the team's client | The merge keeps the stronger role, ranked by the deal's side (team client > team co-client > other side's client > lender/title/attorney > other), so updates continue | R-OWN-24 |
| E351 | Duplicates holding the same home address are merged after this month's home-value note went out | No second vendor request and no second note this month; the next month's prior estimate includes the merged record's history | R-AVM-3 |
| E313 | Google retries a lead (same lead_id) | One contact; the second is duplicate_ignored; both answered 200 {} | R-GADS-2 |
| E314 | Google "Send test data" (is_test) | No contact, inquiry or job; "Test lead received <time>". Until one arrives, the page says Google's test may not reach us | R-GADS-3 |
| E315 | Wrong, retired, missing or non-string Google key | 403 and a tenant-less rejected row, nothing else (Google does not retry 4XX) | R-GADS-1 |
| E316 | Unknown column ids, custom answers or new top-level fields | Known fields mapped; the rest ignored and counted; nothing fails | R-GADS-2 |
| E317 | No usable lead_id, name, phone or email (incl. every phone and email invalid) | Nothing created or stored; counted with its time; 200 {}; "download it from Google Ads" | R-GADS-3 |
| E318 | An id sent as a JSON number above 2^53 | Dropped, never rounded | R-GADS-2 |
| E319 | Source turned off, then on; form hook turned off | Off: 403; on again: the same key works; hook off: the key is retired forever | R-LSRC-2, R-GADS-1, R-GADS-4 |
| E320 | Unknown caller to a tracked number | Contact credited to the source and offered by its rule; no cited text or grant from it; no 60-second reply; the call rings as today | R-TRK-2 |
| E321 | Owned or offered person texts or calls a tracked number | Threaded; no inquiry; owner unchanged | R-TRK-3 |
| E322 | Untied number, off source or released number | Today's unknown-caller path ("Unknown +1…", no source) | R-TRK-3 |
| E323 | Call and text from the same new number at once on a tracked number | One contact, one inquiry (address lock) | R-TRK-2, R-OWN-25 |
| E324 | Tracked call races a signed webhook lead with the same phone and source | No deadlock (one sorted lock call); one contact | R-TRK-2 |
| E325 | Tie a source to a non-tracking number | Refused (DB CHECK; NUMBER_NOT_TRACKING) | R-TRK-1 |
| E326 | Lead email forwarded by hand from a consumer mailbox | Held PERSONAL_MAILBOX; only the forwarded block read; the lead's own address kept; Accept needs no retyping | R-LEM-11 |
| E327 | Allowlisting a consumer mailbox domain, or an older allowlist that has one | Refused; a saved entry is ignored and shown as not allowed | R-LEM-11 |
| E328 | Gmail forwarding confirmation reaches a source address | Held FORWARDING_CONFIRMATION naming who asked; never a lead; a forged copy without the aligned pass gets the ordinary hold | R-LEM-12 |
| E329 | Auto-forwarded portal email | Signature intact ⇒ lead; broken ⇒ DKIM_FAILED; source address only in the envelope recipient ⇒ still routed | R-LEM-12 |
| E330 | leadmetadata tags in the body, or an unknown version | Body tags read with spans; an unknown version is not read | R-LEM-10 |
| E331 | Hostile leadmetadata (markup, controls, bidi, thousands of tags, huge values, escaped or commented tags in the message) | Capped, cleaned, linear; never throws; escaped or commented tags not read | R-LEM-10, R-LEM-8 |
| E332 | A catalog portal without a vendor row sends a lead | Generic when labelled; otherwise held UNKNOWN_FORMAT; nothing routes; the page asks for a sample | R-LEM-9 |
| E333 | Webhook credentials rotated | Old URL and secret refused at once; the deployment secret is refused for a source with its own and kept for one without | R-LSRC-3 |
| E334 | Preview while a round-robin is mid-round | Names who a lead arriving now would be offered to; the cursor is unchanged; with nothing else changed, the next real lead goes to that person | R-LSRC-4 |
| E335 | Agent-forwarding rule sits below a catch-all | Preview names the catch-all; the page offers to move the rule to the top | R-LSRC-5 |
| E336 | Forwarding agent suspended, not accepting or at capacity | Preview names the fallback pond and the reason in words | R-LSRC-4 |
| E337 | Consent text changed on a source with a website chat | New chats show the new text; a visitor holding the old one gets the stale-disclosure answer; earlier inquiries keep citing the old text | R-LSRC-2, R-CONSENT-4, R-INBOX-5 |
| E338 | Two Owners rotate a webhook or Google key at once | Serialized; both 200; only the later key works; each response says when its key was made; never a 500 | R-LSRC-3, R-GADS-4 |
| E339 | Deployment without an inbound domain or a public https address, or with test phone numbers | The page says so per path; nothing is offered that cannot work | R-LSRC-1 |
| E340 | First text "STOP" from a new number to a tracked number | Lead created with no grant; the opt-out recorded exactly as for any unknown texter today; only the confirmation reply | R-TRK-2, R-CONSENT-1 |
| E352 | The source is turned off between the acquire check and the number insert | The number is bought and saved untied; the response says so; nothing fails after a purchase | R-TRK-1 |
| E353 | A reviewer tries to accept a forwarding confirmation (even with a typed phone) | Refused; Discard only | R-LEM-12 |
| E354 | A provider retries a lead email whose first processing failed or crashed | Processed on the retry; one lead; a processed delivery stays a duplicate | R-LSRC-7 |
| E590 | Hand forward whose signature carries the forwarder's email and phone; two such forwards of different leads, both accepted | Neither the forwarder's From address, a member's address or alert phone, nor a team number is kept; two contacts, never one | R-LEM-11 |
| E591 | The Google key lookup fails (pool or statement timeout) | 503, no rejected row; never a 403 | R-GADS-1 |
| E592 | A Google lead with values longer than the intake limits | Truncated to the limits; the lead is created; never a 4XX | R-GADS-2 |
| E593 | Two Owners turn on a source's Google form at once | One 200 with the key; the other 409 GOOGLE_LEAD_FORM_ALREADY_ON with the state | R-GADS-4 |
| E594 | Person in a pond, or left unassigned by an import with no route due, calls or texts a tracked number | One inquiry from the source; routed once by the re-inquiry rules; the call rings as today | R-TRK-3, R-OWN-20 |
| E595 | Pond person texts STOP, opt-out language or a wrong-number reply to a tracked number, in the Body or only in an MMS text part | No inquiry, no routing; the opt-out exactly as today (a text with parts is decided after the parts are read; b9 review sends/F2) | R-TRK-3, R-CONSENT-1, R-TWL-11 |
| E596 | leadmetadata names lead_email twice with different values | Held PARTIAL_FORMAT; nothing routes | R-LEM-10 |
| E597 | A lead email, Google lead, feed lead or webhook post arrives while its source is off | Refused and not kept (a partner-feed lead leaves a skip receipt the page counts; turning the source back on does not replay it); the card says "Off since <time>"; the off confirm said so | R-LSRC-2 |
| E380 | Two sends of one team race for the last unit of today's cap (cap-1 used) | The claim is one `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE … WHERE sends < cap` inside each TX1: the second waits for the first's row lock and re-checks on the new count, so exactly one passes; the other is blocked `DAILY_SEND_CAP_REACHED` with its gate_decisions row. A TX1 that rolls back returns its unit | R-TWL-2 |
| E381 | The cap is reached and a contact texts STOP (the platform is the confirmation sender) | The STOP confirmation / HELP reply is opt-out-exempt and cap-exempt: it goes out and claims nothing | R-TWL-2, R-GATE-10 |
| E382 | A status callback arrives without `From` (Twilio's master account, or a fake) | Accepted: the guard's tenant, else one unrouted row + `WEBHOOK_UNROUTED` and 200 (never 422); a missing `MessageSid` is still 422 | R-TWL-4 |
| E383 | Twilio reports `OptOutType=STOP` for a body our classifier reads as opt-out language ("please stop texting me") or a wrong number | Our classification stands (it already revokes): no second classification, no extra revoke; only a body we read as none/help/start becomes `stop` (`OPTOUTTYPE_STOP`) | R-TWL-7 |
| E384 | `OptOutType=START` with body START, or a body of YES, from an opted-out phone | START is the existing keyword path (it lifts only what R-CONSENT-10 allows); `OptOutType` adds nothing; YES is no keyword and lifts nothing | R-TWL-7 |
| E385 | Several texts from one unverified toll-free number fail with 30032 the same day (from submits and status callbacks) | Each fails `SENDER_NOT_VERIFIED`; the registration is marked not ready once (the first event); one Owner/Admin notice per number per tenant-local day; calls from the number still work | R-TWL-9 |
| E386 | STOP appears only in an MMS text part (the Body is empty or harmless), and the job runs twice | The part revokes like a STOP body (every phone of the contact, R-CONSENT-7), the platform confirms once (dedupe on the message id), the second run finds the part read and does nothing | R-TWL-11 |
| E387 | An MMS text part is larger than 16 KiB, has a NUL, is invalid UTF-8, or the fetch is refused (the kill switch included) | A skipped row (`TOO_LARGE`, `CONTENT_MISMATCH`, `FETCH_FAILED`) and one notice to the conversation's handler: "Part of a text from {name} couldn't be read. Check the conversation before you text them." | R-TWL-11 |
| E388 | An agent's verified alert phone sends an MMS whose text part says STOP | There is no message row for an alert phone: the part revokes the agent's alert texts exactly as the agent keyword path does (Admins told) | R-TWL-11, R-TWL-8 |
| E389 | A text part says START (or YES) after the phone opted out | Ignored: a part only ever adds opt-outs, never lifts one | R-TWL-11 |
| E360 | A closed Residential Lease (ClosePrice = monthly rent) is in the feed, or a lease the vocabulary misses arrives as "Residential" at 2,400.00 | Stored with is_lease where recognised; never a comp; a missed one is in notUsed ("the price looks like a rent or a data error"); with F3-lease, never in a buyer alert: not matched, not sent from a match recorded before the flag (batch 9 stored leases unflagged), a queued digest holding one is canceled (`MLS_LEASE`), and a matched lease makes nobody a nurture follower (batch 10 review ownership-data/F1; an agent's explicit "interested" link still counts); never a competing listing either (core competingSet skips `valuation.isLease`, and the CMA's market context reads `NOT is_lease`), so no CMA context, agent competing view or seller update shows a rent as a list price (batch 10 review ownership-data/F5) | R-CMA-1, R-CMA-2 |
| E361 | Longitude arrives as "-80.1234567" text; latitude is 95; the coordinates are (0,0); YearBuilt is 0; Stories is "1.5"; CloseDate is "2024-02-30" | −80123457 µ°; invalid or zero pairs are null; YearBuilt and the date are null and named unreadable; Stories is stored as 15 tenths; the record is stored; a record the database still refuses is skipped (MLS_RECORD_REFUSED_BY_DB) and the other 499 of its batch are stored | R-CMA-1 |
| E362 | Lot size arrives in acres only, in square metres, or in two fields that disagree; SubdivisionName is "None", "N/A" or "0"; two subdivisions in different towns share a name | Lot size normalized to sq ft, and on disagreement the sq ft field wins and the rest are unreadable; placeholders are stored as null and never form a tier; a same-named subdivision more than 3 mi away in another postal code is never tier 1 | R-CMA-1, R-CMA-2 |
| E363 | ClosePrice on a pending record; Closed without CloseDate; CloseDate after the valuation date | Stored as read; never a comp; never read as a candidate | R-CMA-1, R-CMA-2 |
| E364 | Only 2 eligible sales | No value (INSUFFICIENT_COMPS); the 2 sales are listed | R-CMA-4 |
| E365 | The subject's earlier sale is in the feed under another MLS number, and its address is spelled differently (UnparsedAddress with unit and city, region written out) | Matched by the address key; excluded from comps; shown as the subject's sale history | R-CMA-2 |
| E366 | A team listing is mirrored in the MLS as sold | Counted once; the team row's facts with the MLS close price and size; shown to clients only through clientSoldView | R-MLS-5, R-CMA-2 |
| E367 | A short-sale or REO comp | Not used by default ("not used: short sale"); after the agent includes it, it is adjusted and labelled | R-CMA-2 |
| E368 | A comp with $15,000 of seller concessions | Its adjusted price is lowered by $15,000, never raised | R-CMA-3 |
| E369 | The MLS feed has been stale for 3 days | The agent CMA works with "MLS data as of …"; client share is blocked (FEED_STALE); (V2) a home-value note is a visible skip even though its date is within 35 days | R-CMA-6, R-CMA-7, R-CMA-10, R-MLS-3 |
| E370 | The subject's active listing has InternetAutomatedValuationDisplayYN = N; or a live listing at a near-identical address exists | The agent CMA works; client outputs are blocked (SELLER_WITHHELD) under the default scope; a near match blocks sharing (POSSIBLE_LIVE_LISTING) until the agent confirms it is a different home, and makes automated notes not displayable | R-CMA-7, R-CMA-10 |
| E371 | An existing reviewed pack has no valuation section | The agent CMA is refused in words, naming where the use is recorded (the MLS admin page, Valuation use: module 41's editor sends V1-A's `valuation` body with the stored display pack unchanged; batch 10 review api-ui/F2); the display pack keeps its review state | R-CMA-6 |
| E372 | Rows stored before this release (normalize version 1) are re-read by the backfill with the same ModificationTimestamp | New columns are filled; no alert, no nurture firing, no price history; the incremental feed is read first in every run and freshness is unchanged; a second pass changes nothing; GET /admin/mls says "Filling sold history" until it ends, but only while the sync reads the connection (connected or degraded, and not Spark with `MLS_LIVE_CALLS` false); otherwise it says no sold history is read and why (batch 10 review api-ui/F6) | R-CMA-1, R-MLS-3, R-MLS-6 |
| E373 | The contact's address is not in the MLS history | The agent enters type and size (labelled agent; other facts optional); without coordinates, subdivision, postal and city tiers only; "distance not known" | R-CMA-2, R-CMA-8 |
| E374 | (V2) A past client's address is the subject of an active relisting with InternetAutomatedValuationDisplayYN = N | The monthly note is NOT_DISPLAYABLE (a visible skip) | R-CMA-10, R-AVM-2 |
| E375 | (V2) The seller update finds fewer than 3 comps | "Value range unavailable: not enough comparable sales"; no number | R-CMA-10, R-SELL-2 |
| E376 | (V2) Share with a comp the pack hides from clients | Blocked: "1 of these sales can't be shown to clients…"; after removing it, the share sends the recomputed value | R-CMA-7, R-CMA-9 |
| E377 | (V2) A PATCH after share | CMA_NOT_DRAFT; a new one-tap draft; the shared row unchanged (owner question h) | R-CMA-8 |
| E400 | Declared or sniffed type is not PDF, JPEG or PNG (an SVG, HTML, DOCX or ZIP file, a PDF declared as a JPEG, an empty file) | `DOCUMENT_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED` (422); nothing is stored | R-DOC-5 |
| E401 | A body over the multipart cap, or a file part over 10,000,000 bytes | 413 `PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE` before the body is read, or 413 `DOCUMENT_TOO_LARGE`; no object or document row either way | R-DOC-5 |
| E402 | No virus scanner is configured (production until N11) | Every upload stays pending. Only the uploader and tenant-wide actors see it, with "This platform has no virus scanner yet, so the file stays locked. Nobody can open it until a scanner checks it."; it is never streamed (the Owner included), extracted, shared or re-enqueued | R-DOC-3, R-DOC-7, R-INT-1 |
| E403 | The scanner fails (an outage, a timeout) | The job retries; the document stays pending, never clean; the reconcile re-enqueues a scan pending for over 2 minutes | R-DOC-7 |
| E404 | A password-protected or unscannable file | Blocked (`infected`, final) with "The virus check could not read this file (it may be password-protected), so it was blocked. Upload a copy without a password." (a viewer who cannot read the verdict gets a sentence true for either reason that asks the same); its object is shredded; a second scan changes nothing | R-DOC-7 |
| E405 | Malware in a pending document already on legal hold | Blocked; its object kept locked, never streamed; shredded by the reconcile once the hold is lifted. **VERIFY WITH COUNSEL:** whether a held malicious file must be kept, and whether blocked uploads are records | R-DOC-4, R-DOC-7 |
| E406 | Two people upload a new version of one document at once | One becomes v2; the other gets `DOCUMENT_VERSION_CONFLICT` (the linear-chain index). Its ciphertext stays unreadable because its DEK never committed (an orphan blob; ARCHITECTURE §21.31 residual) | R-DOC-6 |
| E407 | A version across scopes, or of a document the uploader cannot see | `DOCUMENT_VERSION_INVALID` in the database, answered with 404 | R-DOC-6 |
| E408 | A document naming a recording, MMS, consent-evidence or another person's object, another tenant's or a shredded one, or one of the wrong type or size, or one already used | `DOCUMENT_OBJECT_INVALID` (database); the foreign key alone would accept it | R-DOC-6 |
| E409 | A new version of a document shared to the portal | The new version is pending and unshared. The old version stays shared until someone unshares it, and the response says so: "Version N-1 is still shared to the client portal. When this version passes the virus check, share it and stop sharing version N-1." | R-DOC-9 |
| E410 | Unsharing a document already published to a client | Downloads stop at once (`portal.issue_document_stream` returns nothing), but the item stays listed until module 45 retracts it, and the response says so | R-DOC-9 |
| E411 | A TC or ISA shares, or anyone shares a contact-scoped or unclean document | Refused in the API (404 for the TC and the ISA; `DOCUMENT_NOT_SHAREABLE` otherwise) and in the database (`DOCUMENT_SHARE_NOT_ALLOWED`) | R-DOC-1, R-DOC-9 |
| E412 | One contract states two different closing dates | Two claims, side by side, neither preselected (a confirm names its target), each with "This contract states more than one date for this. Check which applies before you confirm one." | R-DOC-8 |
| E413 | The extracted date differs from a confirmed timeline date | Both shown (the claim lists the deal's live dates of its kind). Using the contract's date is the person's move (`due_version` bumped, stale firings skipped; a later move by hand says "Moved by a person from the contract's date."). Keeping the timeline date rejects the claim and leaves the date untouched | R-DOC-8, R-TXN-5 |
| E414 | Double tap on Confirm | One deal date; the replay returns it (one date per claim, database index) | R-DOC-8 |
| E415 | v1's extraction undecided when v2 is uploaded | Once v2 is clean, v1's claims get `DOCUMENT_SUPERSEDED` and v1's extraction is marked superseded; dates already confirmed stay. A pending or blocked v2 changes nothing | R-DOC-8 |
| E416 | A deadline written as a number of days ("10 days after acceptance") | Not proposed, even with a date after it; "Deadlines written as a number of days are not read here; plan them from the acceptance date." | R-DOC-8 |
| E417 | A snippet not on its page, or a quoted day other than the proposed day | `needs_review`; the confirm is `EXTRACTION_CLAIM_NEEDS_REVIEW`; the database refuses the date (`DEAL_DATE_EVIDENCE_INVALID`); the person may dismiss it | R-DOC-8, R-AST-5 |
| E418 | A photo (JPEG or PNG) of a contract; also no PDF reader configured, or a listing or contact document | No extraction; "Photos and scans are not read. Enter the dates by hand." (or "Reading dates from PDFs is not set up on this platform") | R-DOC-8, R-INT-1 |
| E419 | A deal closes | Its clean documents with no retention set get `closed_at + 7 years` within one pass, never shortened or overwritten (an Owner's date stands; a contact document is not stamped). Owner or Admin values outside 3–10 years are refused (`DOCUMENT_RETENTION_OUT_OF_RANGE`), and changes are audited | R-DOC-10 |
| E420 | Voice off, playbook revoked or pack withdrawn during the ring | The DB refuses; Phase 1 voicemail and missed-call card; no assistant row. | R-VOICE-11, R-VOICE-9, R-VOICE-1 |
| E421 | Nobody can be rung with a takeover planned | The assistant answers at once; ai_takeover_at = the decision instant. | R-VOICE-3, R-VOICE-11 |
| E422 | The agent presses 1 before the takeover | No AI; ai_takeover_at stays as the decision record. | R-VOICE-11 |
| E423 | A retried voice fetch or dial-result after the takeover | The same reconnect; no second session, ledger row, handback or transfer outcome. | R-VOICE-11 |
| E424 | Hang-up during the disclosure | caller_ended, one critical handback, no tool rows, no recording kept. | R-VOICE-2, R-VOICE-15 |
| E425 | Pre-answer fallback (disclosure/announcement failed or declined, playback timeout, connect failure) | Voicemail with the notice; fallback_voicemail, answered_at null, kept. | R-VOICE-2, R-VOICE-10 |
| E426 | Vendor error or latency mid-call | Voicemail under the announced basis; the reason in the handback. | R-VOICE-10, R-VOICE-15 |
| E427 | bookSlot commits after the hang-up | The end transaction waits, cancels and pushes; UNCONFIRMED_SLOT. | R-VOICE-16 |
| E428 | Calendar unreachable at the start (no slots) or mid-booking (booking_result false) | The handback says so. | R-VOICE-16, R-VOICE-15 |
| E429 | A tool request before both announcements, an unknown tool, a request after a pre-disclosure end, or the 201st row | Unlogged; seq gaps allowed. | R-VOICE-14 |
| E430 | A ring-time handback exists | The voice result merges into it and its pending enrichment cannot replace the draft; one handback, no missed-call card. Already handled by the agent ⇒ a new voice handback. | R-VOICE-15 |
| E431 | The final status beats the end transaction on a never-answered call | At most one missed-call card plus exactly one handback. | R-VOICE-15 |
| E432 | The owner declines, the whisper times out, or 1 is pressed after the hold | Back to the assistant; critical handback and promise task. | R-VOICE-19, R-VOICE-6 |
| E433 | Hang-up during the hold (seen from ended_at) | caller_ended, never bridged, critical handback. | R-VOICE-19, R-VOICE-15 |
| E434 | No verified alert phone | An allowed transfer ends at once as no_answer. | R-VOICE-19, R-VOICE-6 |
| E435 | Spoofed caller ID | The model knows nothing of the contact; booking and transfer reach only its owner; the handback never asserts who called. | R-VOICE-13 |
| E436 | ai_enabled false, agent unconfigured, fake agent on real telephony, the telephony kill switch off on real telephony, or recording off | No takeover; Phase 1 unchanged. | R-VOICE-12 |
| E437 | Anyone creates a module-10 version (voice off, admin/repos/policy.ts:45-50) or an Admin activates one | Answering stops; GET says why. An Owner re-activating an older voice-on version via module 10 turns it back on under that attestation; GET says so. | R-VOICE-20 |
| E438 | An opt-out said over the disclosure is not processed (R-VOICE-2) | A needs-review note points to the recording; no revoke. | R-VOICE-2, R-VOICE-18 |
| E439 | Two AI calls for one contact at once | Separate sessions and handbacks; bookings serialized by the bookSlot lock (E151). | R-VOICE-16, R-VOICE-15 |
| E440 | An Owner or Admin taps Approve while `platform:ads` is not cleared | `ADS_NOT_CLEARED` (API and DB, for the Owner, an Admin and the system); nothing recorded, no provider call | R-ADS-7 |
| E441 | Counsel reviewed `platform:ads` but the vendor check (`rules.vendor_ready_at`) is not recorded | Still dark: no approval and no planned row; the page says "…it also waits for the vendor check" | R-ADS-7, R-INT-6 |
| E442 | The pack is withdrawn after the tap (while the apply is in flight, or before a readback re-apply) | The planned row fails `PACK_WITHDRAWN`; no budget call is made for it; a call already sent is recorded as it resolves (applied, failed and paused rows, `executed` and `trigger_ref` are never gated); only new approvals and plans are refused | R-ADS-7, R-ADS-10 |
| E443 | The pack is bumped to a new published version that counsel has not reviewed | Dark from the next statement (one published row per key; the gates read it); a pending proposal cannot be approved (`ADS_NOT_CLEARED`) and nothing is planned | R-ADS-7 |
| E444 | An `ad_reallocation` is approved via `/assistant/actions/:id/approve` or outside api/ads, or its payload is edited after approval | The generic route answers 404; never planned: `ADS_APPROVAL_NOT_FROM_ADS` on the ads route, `AD_APPROVAL_FROZEN` for the edit | R-ADS-9 |
| E445 | The Owner sets a new cap version between the proposal and the tap | `ADS_PROPOSAL_STALE`; the proposal is superseded; nothing planned; the next run uses the new version | R-ADS-9, R-ADS-2 |
| E446 | A budget is changed in the Google Ads interface between the proposal and the tap | The mirror takes the new amount; the tap is `ADS_PROPOSAL_STALE` and the proposal superseded | R-ADS-9, R-ADS-12 |
| E447 | The apply call times out | Readback in the hourly reconcile: the planned amount ⇒ applied `READBACK`; the old amount ⇒ one re-apply, marked first in `trigger_ref.reapplied`; a third amount ⇒ failed `PLATFORM_CHANGED` (the mirror takes it); still unresolved after 24 h ⇒ failed `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`. Never a second planned row per approval | R-ADS-10, R-ADS-3 |
| E448 | One change of a run is applied and the other rejected by the platform | Both outcomes recorded; the run is `executed`; the mirror moves only where applied (the failed one counts at its synced budget) | R-ADS-3, R-ADS-10 |
| E449 | A campaign runs on Google Ads but is not managed here (not linked, housing not declared, shared budget, non-USD) | Mirrored as `draft`, counted in the monthly cap, shown as not managed with the reason; its budget is in the "not managed here" total | R-ADS-12, R-ADS-5 |
| E450 | The Google Ads account's zone differs from the tenant's | `ads_sync` months are the account zone's; the note names the zone and the sync time | R-ADS-11 |
| E451 | A lead source has a manual cost and an `ads_sync` cost for the same month | Both are counted by the owner view; the ads page warns that both are counted | R-ADS-11, R-OWNER-5 |
| E452 | Month-to-date spend reaches the cap while `platform:ads` is dark, or a serving campaign was never mirrored because the connection has no lead source | Dark: one alert to every active Owner/Admin ("pause them in Google Ads"), no provider write; the pack withdrawn during a pass: no further pause call, the same alert. Cleared but no lead source (an Admin may clear it after mirroring, b11 sends-gates/F2): the mirrored campaigns are still paused, and the campaigns never mirrored get the alert | R-ADS-5, R-ADS-7 |

| E553 | Home values older than 13 months at the avm.refresh pass | Deleted for that tenant only, except one a scheduled run or a still-proposed/approved draft cites; one cited only by an executed draft goes; another tenant's rows stay; the purge returns its count. | E554 | Someone other than the tenant's system tries to purge home values, or passes an instant | A user, support (even under a grant) or a pre-tenant session is refused `HOME_VALUE_PURGE_SYSTEM_ONLY`; there is no instant parameter; avm.refresh purges even with no AVM vendor configured and never calls the vendor | R-DATA-6 |
| E555 | A person reacts to a team text between a Team Lead's merge and its follow-up | The survivor's agent sees the reaction line quoting the person's own message, the not-an-opt-out line and the do-not-contact offer; after the follow-up the quote is the team's text | R-REACT-6, R-OWN-24 |
| E556 | A merge commits while the reminder pass waits on the loser's contact lock | The pass re-reads recipients under the locks: one update planned for the survivor, none for the loser, `reminderPass.planned = 1`, no merge re-plan needed | R-TXN-4, R-OWN-24 |
| E557 | An approval is refused because the deal date its update cites moved, was completed or waived, or is gone (DRAFT_STALE with `details.reason`) | No "Send anyway" and no re-tap (nothing clears it): Approvals hides Send and says why with Dismiss; the compose box detaches the draft and keeps the text as the agent's own; a feed card says why in place, ending "Dismiss it in Approvals.", with no thread jump. The inbound they-replied case keeps its words (**VERIFY** V9: whether the update plan covers every moved date) | R-TXN-5 |
| E558 | A photo or video text in the thread | Its tag reads MMS (owner question 9); a plain text stays SMS | R-MMS-8 |
| E559 | The compose box's send or upload on a record merged away meanwhile | The web contract of module 22 names `CONTACT_MERGED` (409), which the web already words | R-OWN-24 |
| E560 | Recognised, but no complete home or property address and no K2 address | No lookup and no profile. Panel: "Add the home address to build the profile." (a complete K2 address of any kind is a subject and is listed) | R-HOME-2, R-HOME-5 |
| E561 | The only homeowner tag is `protected_proxy` (even one named like the homeowner tag key) | Not recognised by tag: no lookup, no profile | R-HOME-1 |
| E562 | The record names someone else | `not_matched` (`owner_name_mismatch`): no facts, mailing address, parcel reference or other owner's data; the panel says the record does not name them as owner | R-HOME-3 |
| E563 | "OWNER A & OWNER B", and the contact is one of them | Matched (split on "&"); "LAST FIRST & FIRST2" does not match FIRST2, because the second part is the bare first name (verify_items 14) | R-HOME-3 |
| E564 | An LLC or trust owner, even when the contact has that name | `not_matched` / `missing_data`: "does not name a person as owner"; a person named beside an entity still matches (**VERIFY WITH COUNSEL**) | R-HOME-3 |
| E565 | Several address points or parcels match (or none does) | `ambiguous` (none: `not_available`); nothing kept; the panel says so in words | R-HOME-3, R-HOME-10 |
| E566 | The address is edited between lookup and write | Not written (fenced); due on the next pass, which looks up the new address | R-HOME-6 |
| E567 | Recognition is lost between lookup and write (or the contact is marked do-not-contact or merged meanwhile) | Not written; purged on the next pass | R-HOME-1, R-HOME-6 |
| E568 | The contact becomes do-not-contact | No lookup; the profile is purged on the next pass | R-HOME-1, R-HOME-9 |
| E569 | Merged away | Purged; the survivor builds its own if it has a subject (addresses, inquiries and intent do not move with a merge). A recognised survivor's panel tells every viewer of it (agent, Team Lead, Owner, Admin) that a record was merged into it (read from the merge tree, not the hidden record); an unrecognised survivor shows no panel: recognition does not carry over (R-HOME-1) | R-HOME-9, R-HOME-1 |
| E570 | Erasure | Profiles and facts cascade | R-HOME-9, R-DATA-7 |
| E571 | Address deleted | Its profile and facts cascade | R-HOME-9 |
| E572 | County unreachable (a 5xx, a timeout), or an ArcGIS error at 200 | Old values kept with their dates and retention; the failure is shown ("Couldn't reach the county records on <date>"); 1-day retry; with no profile, a `failed` row with no values; P2 `WORKER_ERROR` (`PUBLIC_RECORDS_SOURCE_FAILING`) at the threshold with no success | R-HOME-6 |
| E573 | Live calls off (the default) | No request, no row; the panel shows the status text | R-HOME-10 |
| E574 | Production unconfigured | No row and no lookup; the purge runs; "Public records are not set up on this platform" | R-HOME-6 |
| E575 | Jasper address | Links only (the county's pages); never looked up | R-HOME-5 |
| E576 | Shared ZIP | Beaufort tried; if `not_available`, the Jasper links too | R-HOME-5 |
| E577 | Outside SC, or ZIP not covered | No county and no lookup: "No county source for this address"; no links | R-HOME-5 |
| E578 | Tokens outside the whitelist (quotes, an over-long name, no house number) | Refused before any request (`address_invalid`); retried after an address edit or 30 days | R-HOME-10 |
| E579 | Redirect, oversize or non-JSON | Failure (`rejected` / `unavailable`); nothing parsed | R-HOME-10 |
| E580 | The payload has an age, phone, lender, instruments or other owners' addresses | Dropped by the parser; never stored | R-HOME-3 |
| E581 | Transfer date in the future or before 1800 (or another malformed value: negative cents, control or bidi characters, an over-long string) | Dropped | R-HOME-4 |
| E582 | No county date, but a team closing the viewer sees | Years owned from the closing, named ("since the team's closing on <date>"); nothing for a viewer who cannot see the deal, or when the county record names someone else | R-HOME-4 |

| E584 | A fact on a non-matched profile, or leaving matched with facts | Refused (guard); the pass deletes the facts first | R-HOME-3 |
| E585 | A viewer without contact-detail access | 404 (a contact that is not recognised is `{ recognized: false }`, and the panel is hidden) | R-HOME-7 |
| E586 | Second pass on the same local date, or budget spent | No lookup; the remaining subjects wait for the next local date | R-HOME-6 |
| E587 | Retention over 366 days, or a row past retention | Refused (CHECK); hidden (policy) and purged by the next pass | R-HOME-9 |

| E601 | The agent edits the draft in another tab between preview and tap | CMA_STALE CONTENT_CHANGED. Nothing is stored or sent, and the preview refetches. | R-CMA-9 |

(c) DATA_OLD. (a) and (c) together: DATA_OLD, as the brief orders the checks (the data date before the sales; batch 13, audit cma-v2a F10). | R-CMA-9, R-MLS-2, R-MLS-3 |
| E603 | The recipient opts out (a) after the preview, or (b) after the share commits but before dispatch | (a) CMA_RECIPIENT_BLOCKED, nothing frozen. (b) Dispatch blocks it (gate_decisions, no provider call); the CMA stays shared, and the view says "The email was not sent: …". | D1, R-GATE-14 |
